## Quebec Fishery Regulations, 1990 {#quebec-fishery-regulations-1990 .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/90-214
:::

[FISHERIES ACT](/eng/acts/F-14)

Registration 1990-03-29

Regulations Respecting Fishing in the Province of Quebec

P.C. 1990-585 1990-03-29
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans, pursuant to sections 8 and
43[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_Document1_e_hq_8135){#fn_Document1_e_hq_8135-ID0EHBAA}
of the [Fisheries Act](/eng/acts/F-14), is pleased hereby to revoke the
Quebec Fishery Regulations, C.R.C., c. 852, and to make the annexed
[Regulations respecting fishing in the Province of
Quebec](/eng/regulations/SOR-90-214), in substitution therefor,
effective April 1, 1990.

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_Document1_e_hq_8135-ID0EHBAA){#fn_Document1_e_hq_8135}R.S., c.
  35 (1st Supp.), s. 7
  :::
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-921862 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations may be
cited as the [Quebec Fishery Regulations,
1990](/eng/regulations/SOR-90-214).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-921864 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In these
  Regulations,

  [[American eel trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[American eel trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trap that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is made of plastic or wire mesh with a minimum
        mesh size of 2.85 cm, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is made of netting with a minimum mesh size of
        5.7 cm; ([cage à anguilles d'Amérique]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing, by means of a line
      or a rod and line to which is attached a hook that may be baited,
      including fly fishing but not including fishing by means of a
      night line; ([pêche à la ligne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area of the Province
      delimited as a fishing area under section 84.1 of An Act
      respecting the conservation and development of wildlife, CQLR, c.
      C-61.1; ([zone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[artificial fly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[artificial fly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hook or combination
      of hooks dressed with silk, tinsel, textiles, fur, feathers or
      other similar materials including, as parts of a fly, metal
      (brass, copper or aluminum) or plastic tubing, straight pin,
      Waddington shaft, non-metal eyes or head but does not include
      flies to which is attached an external device likely to attract
      fish, baited flies, flies with a spinning or undulating device and
      weights that cause the fly to sink; ([mouche
      artificielle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[artificial lure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[artificial lure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a spoon, simulated
      minnow, artificial fly or any other device made of feathers,
      fibres, rubber, wood, metal, plastic or similar materials that has
      one or more hooks; ([leurre artificiel]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Assistant Deputy Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Assistant Deputy Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2001-51, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[bait trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bait trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fishing device that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has no wings or leaders,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is made of netting or plastic or wire mesh,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is fastened to hoops or frames,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has a maximum length of 60 cm and a maximum
        width of 25 cm, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is equipped with funnel-shaped openings, the
        smallest diameter of which does not exceed
        2.5 cm; ([bourolle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[box net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[box net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fishing device that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has one or more wings or leaders,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} terminates in a box-shaped net, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is used to catch fish without enmeshing
        them; ([filet à réservoir]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Centre d'étude et de recherche Manicouagan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Centre d'étude et de recherche
      Manicouagan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2001-51, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Chief of the Wildlife Conservation Service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Chief of the Wildlife Conservation
      Service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2001-51, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in relation to an
      individual means a person who is cohabiting with the individual in
      a conjugal relationship, having so cohabited for a period of at
      least one year; ([conjoint de fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"});

  [[communal wildlife area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[communal wildlife area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a territory
      established by an order of the Minister and given to lease
      exclusive fishing rights for communal purposes; ([aire faunique
      communautaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[controlled zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[controlled zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[zec]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area of the Province so
      designated under An Act respecting the conservation and
      development of wildlife, CQLR, c. C-61.1; ([zone d'exploitation
      contrôlée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}ou[zec]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[county]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[county]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an electoral district
      described in the Territorial Division Act of Quebec, CQLR,
      c. D-11; ([comté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[crayfish trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[crayfish trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trap that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} does not have wings or leaders,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is funnel or box-shaped, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is made of netting or plastic or wire
        mesh; ([casier à écrevisses]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who works in the
      Wildlife Sector of the Ministry, and who occupies a managerial
      position with respect to fisheries; ([directeur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Duchesnay Sanctuary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Duchesnay Sanctuary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/91-336, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[ecological reserve]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ecological reserve]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area of the
      Province so designated under the Natural Heritage Conservation
      Act, CQLR, c. C-61.01; ([réserve écologique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fish trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fish trap]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trap that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} does not have any wings or leaders,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is made of netting or plastic or wire mesh,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is fastened on hoops or frames,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is equipped with openings, the largest
        dimension of which does not exceed 2.5 cm, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is used to catch fish without enmeshing
        them; ([nasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fly fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fly fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing by means of a
      fly line mounted on a rod designed specifically for that type of
      fishing and to which are attached artificial flies; ([pêche à la
      mouche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a shaft with one or more points
      and includes an artificial lure; ([hameçon]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[hoop net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hoop net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fishing device that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consists of a series of interconnected conical
        pockets narrowing at equal distances to a point,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is equipped with one or more leaders or one or
        more wings,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is made of netting or plastic or wire mesh, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is fastened to hoops or
        frames; ([verveux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Indian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Indian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/99-264, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Inuk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Inuk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/99-264, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Lake Saint-Pierre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Lake Saint-Pierre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/97-203, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Lake Saint-Pierre archipelago]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Lake Saint-Pierre archipelago]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/97-203, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[landing net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[landing net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bag-shaped net that is
      mounted on a frame such that any dimension of the net does not
      exceed 90 cm; ([épuisette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[large]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[large]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of an Atlantic
      salmon, 63 cm or more in length; ([grand]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[leader]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[leader]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vertical panel that is
      attached to the front of fishing gear in order to lead fish
      towards the opening in the gear; ([guideau]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an Atlantic salmon, the distance
        measured in a straight line from the tip of the snout to the
        fork of the tail, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any other fish, the distance
        measured in a straight line from the tip of the snout to the tip
        of the caudal fin; ([longueur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a document issued under
      these Regulations conferring on the holder the privilege to do the
      things described in the document, subject to the [Fisheries
      Act](/eng/acts/F-14), these Regulations and the terms and
      conditions prescribed in the licence; ([permis]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[lift net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lift net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is made of netting or plastic or wire mesh,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is fastened to a frame that is usually
        square-shaped,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is attached to a rope,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} does not exceed 1.3 m in its fully extended
        length, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} has a maximum mesh size of
        2.5 cm; ([carrelet]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[maximum size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[maximum size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to the
      hook of an artificial fly, a distance between the point of the
      hook and the shaft not exceeding

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 17 mm for a single hook,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 13 mm for a double hook, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 7 mm for a triple hook; ([taille
        maximale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mesh size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect to netting, the distance between the
        extreme angles of a single mesh measured inside the knots after
        being stretched to form two parallel straight lines, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of plastic or wire mesh, the
        greatest distance across the opening of the
        mesh; ([maillage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the issuance of any of the
        commercial fishing licences listed in subitems 1(6) to (18) and
        (20), column 1, of Part 2 of Schedule 5, the Quebec Minister of
        Agriculture, Fisheries and Food,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the issuance of a commercial
        fishing licence listed in subitem 1(19), column 1, of Part 2 of
        Schedule 5,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the licence is in respect of bait fish,
          the Minister responsible in the Province for the application
          of An Act respecting the conservation and development of
          wildlife, CQLR, c. C-61.1, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the licence is not in respect of bait
          fish, the Quebec Minister of Agriculture, Fisheries and Food,
          and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in all other cases, the Minister responsible in
        the Province for the application of An Act respecting the
        conservation and development of wildlife, CQLR,
        c. C-61.1; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Ministry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Ministry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Ministry responsible
      for fishing in the Province; ([ministère]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[night line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[night line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a line to which hooks are
      attached at intervals, but does not include a line used in
      angling; ([ligne dormante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[outfitter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[outfitter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, for the purposes of these
      Regulations, a territory established by an Order of the Minister
      and given to lease exclusive fishing rights to a company for
      developing the use of wildlife
      resources; ([pourvoirie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[park]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[park]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area of the Province so
      designated under the Parks Act of Quebec, CQLR,
      c. P-9; ([parc]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pound net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pound net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fishing device that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is equipped with one or more leaders and one or
        more wings,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is made of netting, plastic or wire mesh or
        fascines, and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is used to catch fish without enmeshing
        them; ([trappe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Province of
      Quebec; ([province]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means An Act
      respecting the conservation and development of wildlife, CQLR,
      c. C-61.1, the Natural Heritage Conservation Act, CQLR,
      c. C-61.01, An Act respecting commercial fishing and commercial
      harvesting of aquatic plants, CQLR, c. P-9.01, the Parks Act,
      CQLR, c. P-9, or any regulations made under those
      Acts; ([législation provinciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Regional Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Regional Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2001-51, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
      Act respecting the Conservation and Development of Wildlife of
      Quebec, CQLR, c. C-61.1; ([résident]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Saint-François Bay]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Saint-François Bay]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/97-203, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[salmon pool]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[salmon pool]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place in a salmon
      river identified as a salmon pool by means of posted
      notices; ([fosse à saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[salmon river]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[salmon river]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a river, or a part of a
      river, that is set out in Schedule 6; ([rivière à
      saumon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net that hangs vertically in
      the water with floats attached at the top and weights attached at
      the bottom, the ends of which are brought together or hauled to
      catch fish without enmeshing them; ([seine]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[size]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2008-322, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[small]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[small]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of an Atlantic
      salmon, 30 cm in length or longer and less than 63 cm in
      length; ([petit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Société]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Société]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2005-269, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[sport fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sport fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing for
      recreational purposes or for the personal use of the person who
      catches the fish; ([pêche sportive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[spouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spouse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/95-496, s.
      1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[tailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tailer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that consists of a
      snare attached to the end of a handle that ensnares fish by
      gripping them around the tail; ([serre-queue]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[tidal waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} those parts of the Gulf of St. Lawrence and the
        Baie des Chaleurs between the coastline of Quebec and an
        imaginary line commencing at the terminus of the Labrador-Quebec
        boundary in the Gulf of St. Lawrence, thence along a rhumb-line
        to a point at Latitude 49°25′N., Longitude 60°00′W.; thence
        along a rhumb-line to a point at Latitude 47°02′18″N., Longitude
        60°45′W.; thence along a rhumb-line to a point at Latitude
        47°08′25″N., Longitude 64°01′10″W.; thence due north to a point
        at Latitude 48°12′15″N., Longitude 64°02′10″W.; thence following
        the Quebec-New Brunswick boundary in the Baie des Chaleurs to
        the Campbellton Bridge,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that part of the St. Lawrence River downstream
        from an imaginary line drawn from a point at Latitude
        47°01′57″N., Longitude 70°48′40″W. (Pointe-aux-Prêtres) to a
        point at Latitude 46°56′06″N., Longitude 70°44′11″W.
        (Berthier-sur-Mer),

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that part of the Saguenay River downstream from
        the Dubuc Bridge at Chicoutimi, and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that part of the York River downstream from the
        Gaspé Bridge; ([eaux à marée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trammel net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trammel net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a weighted net made of a
      series of three juxtaposed pieces of webbing with the centre web
      having a smaller mesh size than the side
      webs; ([filet-trémail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trap net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fishing device that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has one or more wings or leaders,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} terminates in a bag-shaped net,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is attached to a stake,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} floats with the tide, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} catches fish without enmeshing them; ([filet à
        poche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[weighted core line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[weighted core line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fly line that,
      when folded firmly and then released, remains folded; ([ligne à
      coeur métallique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[weighted down line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[weighted down line]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fly line to
      which an external weight is attached; ([ligne
      lestée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wildlife reserve]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wildlife reserve]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a park, wildlife
      sanctuary or controlled zone established under provincial
      legislation; ([territoire faunique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wildlife sanctuary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wildlife sanctuary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an area of the
      Province so designated under the Act respecting the Conservation
      and Development of Wildlife of Quebec, CQLR, c. C-61.1; ([réserve
      faunique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vertical panel made of wire
      mesh or netting, attached to one or both sides of fishing gear and
      used to lead fish towards the fishing gear or to catch
      fish. ([aile]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to a species or
  group of species of fish by its common name as set out in column 1 of
  Schedule 3 shall be construed as a reference to the species or group
  of species of fish whose scientific name is set out in column 2.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a period is set out in these Regulations, the
  period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless otherwise specified, begins at 00:01 h on
    the first day set out and ends at 24:00 h on the last day set out;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} begins and ends in the same calendar year, except
    that where it is stated to end on a day in a month that occurs
    earlier in the calendar year than the month in which it begins, the
    period begins in one calendar year and ends in the next calendar
    year.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, the weight of
  fish

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that have only their entrails removed shall be
    deemed to be the weight of the remaining fish plus one quarter of
    that weight;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that have only their entrails and head removed
    shall be deemed to be the weight of the remaining fish plus two
    thirds of that weight; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that are filleted shall be deemed to be the weight
    of the fillets plus two and one-half times that weight.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-921987 .Part}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsections (2) and (3), these Regulations apply in respect of the
  management and control of fishing for freshwater fish and anadromous
  and catadromous species of fish in the waters of the Province and in
  tidal waters.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply in respect of fishing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the waters of a national park of Canada; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any waters or facilities of an aquaculture site,
    a fishing pond, a breeding pond or a fish-tank for baitfish within
    the meaning of An Act respecting the conservation and development of
    wildlife, CQLR, c. C-61.1.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The fishing restrictions established by these
  Regulations do not apply to employees of the Ministry while performing
  their duties.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Sections 7 to 12 and 16 to 18 do not apply to a
  licence referred to in item 2, column 1, of Part 2 of Schedule 5.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Except for this section and section 2, subsections
  4(3), (3.1) and (4) and sections 5, 10 and 23, these Regulations do
  not apply with respect to fishing and related activities carried out
  under the authority of a licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal
  Fishing Licences Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

::: HistoricalNote

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-921998 .Part}

### [Variation of Close Times, Fishing Quotas and Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-921999 .Subheading}

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister or a Director may vary close times, fishing quotas, fishing
  gear or equipment or limits on the length or weight of fish applicable
  to sport fishing that are fixed in respect of an Area under these
  Regulations so that the variation applies in respect of that Area or
  any portion of that Area.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister or a Director may vary any close time
  applicable to commercial fishing under these Regulations in respect of
  the waters referred to in subsection 3(1) or to any portion of them.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may vary any close time fixed by the
  [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332) in respect of species of
  fish or waters referred to in subsection 3(1) so that the variation
  applies to all of the waters or to any portion of them.

- [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Close times varied under subsections (1) to (3) are
  deemed to also vary the corresponding open season where applicable.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The authorized person making a variation under
  subsection (1), (2) or (3), shall give notice of it to the persons
  affected or likely to be affected by it using one or more of the
  following methods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} broadcasting the notice over a radio or television
    station that broadcasts in the Area or in the vicinity of the Area
    affected by the variation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} posting the notice in the Area or in the vicinity
    of the Area affected by the variation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} having a fishery officer, a fishery guardian or a
    representative of the Ministry give oral notice of the variation to
    those persons;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} transmitting or posting the notice by electronic
    means;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} publishing the notice in a newspaper that is
    circulated in the vicinity of the Area affected by the variation;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} publishing the notice in the [Gazette officielle du
    Québec]{.otherLang lang="fr"}; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} publishing the notice in a newsletter or guide that
    deals with sport fishing or commercial fishing in Quebec, published
    by the Ministry or by the Quebec Ministry of Agriculture, Fisheries
    and Food.

::: HistoricalNote

### [Prohibitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922015 .Subheading}

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsections (2) to (4), no person shall fish unless the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is the holder of a licence referred to in Schedule
    5 and issued by the Minister or a Director and fishes in accordance
    with the conditions of that licence; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is the holder of a licence issued under the
    [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332) and fishes in accordance
    with the conditions of that licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may fish without a licence if the person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a resident engaged in sport fishing for Atlantic
    tomcod or rainbow smelt in the St. Lawrence River and its
    tributaries downstream from the Laviolette Bridge (Trois-Rivières
    Bridge);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a resident engaged in sport fishing, for species
    other than Atlantic salmon, in Area 21 or in that part of any river
    in Area 1 that is situated downstream from Highway 132, except for
    the trunk part between Sainte-Flavie and Matapédia;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a resident engaged in sport fishing on the days and
    in the waters designated by the Minister or a Director for the
    purposes of promoting that activity;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a resident engaged in sport fishing for freshwater
    shellfish or freshwater crustaceans; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a resident under 18 years of age who is engaged in
    sport fishing and who is in possession of a certificate attesting to
    their participation in the Quebec Wildlife Foundation's [Pêche en
    herbe]{.otherLang lang="fr"} program or the Ministry's [Relève à la
    pêche]{.otherLang lang="fr"} program.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person may fish under the licence of a licence
  holder, or without a licence when the person fishes under the
  supervision of a holder of the appropriate licence, if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is under 18 years of age and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is the child of the holder of a licence referred
      to in Part 1 of Schedule 5,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is the child of the spouse or common-law partner
      of the holder of a licence referred to in Part 1 of Schedule 5,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} fishes under the supervision of a person 18
      years of age or over who is the holder of a licence referred to in
      Part 1 of Schedule 5, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} fishes under the supervision of the spouse or
      common-law partner of the holder of a licence referred to in item
      1 or 3 of Part 1 of Schedule 5;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is between 18 and 24 years of age, holds a valid
    student card and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is the child of the holder of a licence referred
      to in Part 1 of Schedule 5,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is the child of the spouse or common-law partner
      of the holder of a licence referred to in Part 1of Schedule 5,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} fishes under the supervision of a person 18
      years of age or over who is the holder of a licence referred to in
      Part 1 of Schedule 5, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} fishes under the supervision of the spouse or
      common-law partner of the holder of a licence referred to in item
      1 or 3 of Part 1 of Schedule 5; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is the spouse or common-law partner of the holder
    of a licence referred to in item 1 or 3 of Part 1 of Schedule 5.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person may fish without a licence if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engages in sport fishing in the boundary waters of
    the Ristigouche and Patapédia salmon rivers and holds, and fishes in
    accordance with the conditions of, a licence for sport fishing
    issued by New Brunswick; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} engages in sport fishing in Area 25, in Clarice
    (Montbray Township), Labyrinthe (Dasserat Township) or Raven (Dufay
    Township) lakes or in that part of Lake Saint-François that is
    located west of a line drawn from Beaudette Point on the north shore
    to Saint-Louis Point on the south shore and holds, and fishes in
    accordance with the conditions of, a licence for sport fishing
    issued by Ontario.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a person referred to in paragraph (2)(c) or (e)
  catches an Atlantic salmon, that person shall without delay return the
  fish to the waters in which it was caught and, if the fish is alive,
  release it in a manner that causes the least harm to the fish.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The persons referred to in subsections (3) and (4)
  are subject to the conditions of the applicable licence under the
  authority of which they are fishing.

**[[5.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish on
an ecological reserve.

**[[5.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall possess
any fish that has been caught in contravention of these Regulations.

### [Restricted Waters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922048 .Subheading}

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** No person other than an
[[Indian]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of the
[Indian Act](/eng/acts/I-5) shall fish in any part of the Betsiamites
River set out in item 7 of Schedule 6.

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), no person shall fish for sucker, non-anadromous
  whitefish, sturgeon, mooneye, goldeye or burbot in Area 22, 23 or 24.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may fish in the Areas and for the species
  referred to in subsection (1) where the person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a beneficiary under section 10 of the Act
    respecting hunting and fishing rights in the James Bay and New
    Quebec territories of Quebec, CQLR, c. D-13.1; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a resident of a part of the Northeastern Quebec
    region lying below the 55th parallel as demarcated in Schedule 4 of
    that Act, who is engaged in sport fishing by angling in that part.

### [Fishing Method Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922057 .Subheading}

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish by
means of one or more hooks used in such a manner as to pierce or hook a
fish in any part of the body. This restriction does not apply in the
event that a fish has taken the hook in its mouth.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 3(F)
- SOR/2008-322, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** No person shall engage in
fishing, other than angling, anywhere that is less than 500 m downstream
from any point on the mouth of a salmon river included in Area 18, 19,
20 or 28, or the mouth of a salmon river on the north shore of the
St. Lawrence River or the Gulf of St. Lawrence included in Area 21.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 4
- SOR/99-264, s. 5
- SOR/2008-322, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** No person shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} adjust or attempt to adjust the flow of a dam for the
  purpose of fishing or to facilitate fishing; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} deposit or install, in any water or on its bed, any
  material in order to divert or slow a water current or divert, slow or
  halt the movement of fish for the purpose of fishing or to facilitate
  fishing.

### [Fishing Gear Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922067 .Subheading}

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** No person shall have in
the person's possession a net, hoop net or night line unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person holds the appropriate licence set out in
  Schedule 5;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is licensed under provincial legislation
  to operate a fishing pond or facilities for breeding fish or keeping
  bait fish; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the net, hoop net or night line is kept in a building
  or in a vehicle other than a boat.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-391, s. 2
- SOR/2008-322, s. 6
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 64(F)](#1294976-1295477){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294976-1295477.html"
  role="button"}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall have in their possession any fishing gear within 100 m of any
  water where the use of that gear is prohibited under these
  Regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fishing gear that is located in a building or in a
    vehicle other than a boat; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} persons who are crossing or moving alongside water
    to fish in other waters where the use of that gear is authorized.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(b), when the gear
  is a hook other than an artificial fly, it must not be attached to the
  line, and when the person is also in possession of a rod, it must be
  rendered inoperative by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} disassembling it into sections;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} removing the reel from the rod; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} storing the rod in a closed case.

::: HistoricalNote

### [Fish Tagging Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922085 .Subheading}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person catches and retains a fish and the person is required, under
  the terms of the person's licence, to tag the fish, the person shall
  immediately affix to it, in the manner set out in the licence, a valid
  tag issued with the person's licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), every person who catches
  and retains an Atlantic salmon shall immediately affix to the fish one
  of the valid tags that was issued with the person's licence in the
  sequence that is indicated on the tags.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An Atlantic salmon that is caught and retained shall
  be tagged by the person who hooked it with a valid tag issued with
  that person's licence.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (1) to (3), a tag is
  valid if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it has not been used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it has not been modified or altered in any manner
    that renders any information on it illegible; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it can be affixed, in the manner indicated in
    subsection (2), to the fish that has been caught and retained.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Except in the circumstances described in subsection
  (7), no person shall possess a fish that was caught and retained under
  a licence that requires the fish to be tagged, and no person shall
  possess an Atlantic salmon, unless the fish or salmon is tagged in
  accordance with subsection (1), (2) or (3), as applicable.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Except in the circumstances described in subsection
  (7), no person shall possess

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an Atlantic salmon that came from another province
    or country, or a sturgeon that came from another province or country
    and that was caught in accordance with a commercial fishing licence,
    unless,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is tagged in accordance with the laws of that
      province or country,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a tag of a type approved by the Minister or a
      Director has been affixed to it in the manner described in
      subsection (2), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} it is being transported through the province to
      another province or another country;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an Atlantic salmon that was obtained from the
    Ministry unless a tag of a type approved by the Ministry has been
    affixed to it in the manner described in subsection (2).

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2001-51, s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} No person shall remove a tag that is affixed in
  accordance with this section except at the time the fish is prepared
  for human consumption.

### [Use and Possession of Fish for Bait]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922105 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2021-115, s. 64(F)

\]
:::

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, in any waters where fishing with bait fish is not
  permitted and within a radius of 100 m from such waters,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} use fish for bait; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} possess bait fish other than inside a
    dwelling-house.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who is fishing for bait fish under a
  commercial fishing licence listed in any of subitems 1(1) to (5) and
  (19), column 1, of Part 2 of Schedule 5, or who is fishing for bait
  fish in accordance with section 29, is not considered to possess bait
  fish for the purposes of subsection (1).

### [Release of Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922113 .Subheading}

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** Every person who catches
a fish

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} at a time or place where fishing for that fish is
  prohibited,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} by a method or with fishing gear the use of which is
  prohibited for that fish,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the possession or retention of which is prohibited,

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is an Atlantic salmon, and that is hooked or
  pierced in any part of the body other than the mouth,

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} under the licence referred to in paragraph 1(d)
  or (i) of Part 1 of Schedule 5, or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an Atlantic salmon, under the licence
  referred to in paragraph 2(c) or (f) of Part 1 of Schedule 5,

shall without delay return the fish to the waters in which it was caught
and, if the fish is alive, release it in a manner that causes the least
harm to the fish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 8
- SOR/2003-176, s. 5
- SOR/2004-64, s. 1(E)
- SOR/2008-322, ss. 10, 34
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[16.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16.1 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
voluntarily releases a fish caught during a period when the retention of
such a fish is authorized shall do so in a manner that causes the least
harm to the fish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2008-322, s. 11
:::

### [Spoilage of Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922127 .Subheading}

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** No person who catches and
retains a fish suitable for human consumption shall let the fish spoil.

### [Shipment of Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922129 .Subheading}

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if the
  sale of fish of a species is prohibited by provincial legislation, no
  person shall ship out of the Province any fish of that species that is
  caught by sport fishing.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may take out of the Province fish that the
  person has caught or been given.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-496, s. 8
- SOR/99-264, s. 8
:::

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Licensing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-922135 .Part}

### [Issue of Licences and Tags]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922136 .Subheading}

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** The Minister or a
Director may issue

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licence referred to in Part 1 of Schedule 5 on
  receipt of an application; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a licence referred to in column 1 of Part 2 of
  Schedule 5 on receipt of an application and payment of the applicable
  fee set out in column 2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 9
- SOR/2001-51, s. 7
- SOR/2003-176, s. 6
- SOR/2005-269, s. 52
- SOR/2008-322, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/99-264,
s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

### [Terms and Conditions of Licence]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922143 .Subheading}

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the proper management and control of fisheries, the Minister or a
  Director, as the case may be, may specify in a licence any term or
  condition concerning one or more of the following matters:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the species and quantity of fish that are permitted
    to be taken or transported;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the age, sex, stage of development, length and
    weight of fish that are permitted to be taken or transported;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the waters in which fishing is permitted;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the period during which fishing is permitted or
    fish may be transported;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the locations from or to which fish may be
    transported;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the fishing vessels from which or to which fish may
    be transhipped;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the fishing vessel permitted to be used, and the
    person or persons permitted to operate it;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the size, type and quantity of fishing gear and
    equipment permitted and the manner in which they are to be used;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the location where fishing gear may be installed;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the distance required to be kept between fishing
    gear;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the fishing gear or fishing
    vessels shall be identified;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} the place and the time at which fish may be
    unloaded from a fishing vessel;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} the method whereby the fish may be unloaded from a
    fishing vessel and the method of determining their length or weight;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} the registers that a licence holder must keep of
    fishing activities undertaken under the licence or the sale or
    transportation of fish taken under the licence, as well as the way
    in which the registers shall be kept, their form, the frequency with
    which and the person to whom they shall be presented, and the period
    for which they must be retained;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} the identification and tagging of fish to determine
    its origin, its length or its weight, and the way in which the tags
    issued with the licence are to be recovered;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} the registration of the fish caught or transported;
    and

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} the separation, on board the fishing vessel, of
    fish according to species.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the proper management and control of fisheries,
  the Minister or a Director, as the case may be, may specify in a
  licence set out in subitem 2(1) or (2), column 1, of Part 2 of
  Schedule 5 any term or condition concerning one or more of the
  following matters:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the persons authorized to fish under the authority
    of the licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which fish caught for scientific,
    educational or management purposes shall be kept, released,
    displayed, used, transported or disposed of; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which scientific, educational,
    management and other data collected are to be reported.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the conservation and protection
  of fish, the Minister or a Director, as the case may be, may amend any
  conditions of a licence.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A notice of any amendment referred to in
  subsection (3) shall be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sent to the licence holder by registered mail; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} personally delivered to the licence holder by a
    fishery officer.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} An amendment referred to in subsection (3) is
  effective from the time the licence holder receives the notice
  referred to in subsection (4).

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} A notice referred to in subsection (4) forms part of
  the licence to which it relates, and the licence holder shall, on
  receipt of the notice, attach it to the licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-392, s. 5
- SOR/99-264, s. 10
- SOR/2001-51, s. 8
- SOR/2001-438, s. 4
- SOR/2005-269, s. 52
- SOR/2008-322, s. 13
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 6(E)](#1294849-1295321){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294849-1295321.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-21-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  person, while engaged in fishing, shall have in their possession and,
  on the request of a fishery officer or fishery guardian, shall
  immediately produce, as the case may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their licence or their valid commercial fisher's
    identification certificate, if applicable, issued under
    subsection 49(2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} their valid student card and the licence of the
    person under which they are fishing, if they are fishing under
    subparagraph 5(3)(b)(i) or (ii); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the licence of the person under which they are
    fishing, if they are fishing under paragraph 5(3)(c).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person who is a resident does not have a
  document required under subsection (1) in their possession, the person
  shall produce the document to a fishery officer or fishery guardian
  within seven days after the date of the request.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-496, s. 9
- SOR/98-218, s. 5
- SOR/99-264, s. 11
- SOR/2001-51, s. 9
- SOR/2003-176, s. 7
- SOR/2008-322, s. 14
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  a licence expires on March 31 following the date of issue unless
  otherwise provided in the licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence referred to in paragraph 2(a), (b), (d)
  or (e) of Part 1 of Schedule 5 expires on the earliest of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the holder no longer possesses a
    valid tag issued with the licence,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the Atlantic salmon fishing
    period comes to an end, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date referred to in subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A licence referred to in paragraph 2(c) or (f) of
  Part 1of Schedule 5 expires when the Atlantic salmon fishing period
  comes to an end.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-203, s. 6
- SOR/2003-176, s. 7
- SOR/2004-64, s. 2
- SOR/2008-322, ss. 15, 34
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall buy or have

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the same year, more than one licence set out in
    paragraph 2(a) or (d) of Part 1 of Schedule 5, unless the licence is
    lost or stolen;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person has already bought or already has a
    licence set out in paragraph 2(a) or (d) of Part 1 of Schedule 5 for
    a year, a licence set out in paragraph 2(b) or (e) of that Part;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the same day, more than one licence set out in
    paragraph 2(b) or (e) of Part 1 of Schedule 5; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a licence set out in paragraph 2(b) or (e) of Part
    1 of Schedule 5 if the person has already caught and retained in the
    year a number of Atlantic salmon equal to the quota set out in
    section 36.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph 24(1)(c), the holder of a licence
  set out in paragraph 2(b) or (e) of Part I of Schedule 5 may, if that
  licence has expired on a previous day under paragraph 23(2)(a), buy
  another licence of the same type for the next day.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-203, s. 7
- SOR/2008-322, s. 16
- SOR/2018-55, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence is invalid where it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is issued based on a false declaration;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contains false or misleading information;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is modified or altered in any manner that renders
    any information on the licence illegible;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} does not bear the signature of its holder; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} does not contain the information for which space is
    provided on the licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2003-176, s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-203, s. 8
- SOR/2003-176, s. 8
- [SOR/2024-198, s. 7](#1489246-1492861){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1489246-1492861.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[25.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The licences referred to in item 1
  of Part 1of Schedule 5 are not valid in salmon rivers during the
  Atlantic salmon fishing periods except in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Jacques-Cartier River referred to
    in item 41 of Schedule 6 that are between the Bird Dam at Pont-Rouge
    (46°44′55″N., 71°42′40″W.) and the bridge situated at the south
    entrance of the Valcartier military base (46°54′01″N., 71°30′40″W.);
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the aux Rochers River referred to in
    item 89 of Schedule 6 that are between a straight line joining the
    point 50°01′05″N., 66°52′28″W. to the point 50°01′00″N., 66°52′20″W.
    and the downstream side of the bridge over the western branch of the
    river linking Port-Cartier-Ouest to Port-Cartier (Petit quai
    sector).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The licences referred to in item 2 of Part 1 of
  Schedule 5 are also valid for fishing for species other than Atlantic
  salmon in salmon rivers during the Atlantic salmon fishing periods.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-438, s. 5

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Sport Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-922218 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922219 .Subheading}

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of sport fishing.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-264, s. 12
:::

### [Fishing Method Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922223 .Subheading}

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 28,
29 and 31, no person engaged in sport fishing shall fish for or catch
and retain a species of fish set out in column 1 of Schedule 1 in waters
set out in column 2

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} during the close time set out in column 4; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} during a period other than the close time referred to
  in paragraph (a), if they fish using the gear or method set out in
  column 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 10
- SOR/98-218, s. 6
- SOR/99-264, s. 13
- SOR/2008-322, s. 18
- [SOR/2024-198, s. 8](#1489248-1492863){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1489248-1492863.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[27.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
engage in sport fishing, other than angling, in the waters of Areas 17,
22, 23 and 24.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-198, s. 8](#1489248-1492869){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1489248-1492869.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** No person shall engage in
sport fishing, other than by angling, for

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Atlantic salmon and landlocked salmon;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} muskellunge and tiger muskellunge;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} lake trout and splake trout; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} sturgeon.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 11
- SOR/2008-322, s. 34
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 8](#1294853-1295327){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294853-1295327.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for bait fish unless they hold a licence referred to
  in item 1 of Part 1 of Schedule 5 or are fishing under the authority
  of such a licence and they fish using not more than one lift net or
  not more than three bait traps

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in waters where and during a period when the use of
    bait fish is permitted; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any of Areas 1 to 29 other than during the close
    time, which begins on April 1 and ends on March 31.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), the holder of the
  licence and any person fishing under the authority of the licence
  shall not use more than one lift net or more than three bait traps at
  any one time.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use a bait trap referred to in
  subsection (1) that is not under the person's immediate supervision
  unless the name, address and licence number of the licence holder are
  on the trap.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 12
- SOR/99-264, s. 14
- SOR/2003-176, s. 9(F)
- SOR/2008-322, s. 19
- [SOR/2024-198, s. 9](#1489250-1492870){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1489250-1492870.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall, while angling, use

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} more than one line at a time or, if the use of more
    than one line for angling is permitted, more than the number of
    lines permitted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a line that is not closely and constantly attended
    by the person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a line that is equipped with more than three hooks,
    except when fishing for rainbow smelt in Area 21, when fishing for
    any species of fish in Area 25 or when fishing for any species of
    fish in that part of the waters of Lake Saint-François located in
    Area 8 and west of a line drawn from Beaudette Point on the north
    shore to Saint-Louis Point on the south shore;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a line that is equipped with more than four hooks
    in Area 25 or in that part of the waters of Lake Saint-François
    located west of a line drawn from Beaudette Point on the north shore
    to Saint-Louis Point on the south shore;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in a salmon river during a period when Atlantic
    salmon fishing is permitted, a line with more than one single or
    double hook;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in a salmon river during a period when Atlantic
    salmon fishing is not permitted, a line whose hook or combination of
    hooks has a total of more than three points; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in waters reserved for fly fishing, a line whose
    hook or combination of hooks has a total of more than three points.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the use of more than one line for angling is
  permitted, each line used by a person referred to in
  paragraph 5(3)(a), (b) or (c) is considered to be used by the holder
  of the licence.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in angling and in fly fishing
  simultaneously.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/94-392, s. 6
- SOR/95-496, ss. 10 to 12

  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as set out in Schedule 1, no person shall fish other than by fly
  fishing in a salmon river referred to in Schedule 6 or in waters
  reserved for fly fishing.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as set out in Schedule 1, no person shall fish
  in the waters referred to in subsection (1) by means of an artificial
  fly that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} attached to a weighted core line or a weighted down
    line;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} baited; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} equipped with a single, double or triple hook that
    is larger than the maximum size.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person engaged in fly fishing in a salmon river
  shall use

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} more than one artificial fly when Atlantic salmon
    fishing is authorized in that river; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} more than two artificial flies when Atlantic salmon
    fishing is prohibited in that river.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person engaged in fly fishing in waters reserved
  for fly fishing shall use more than two artificial flies.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 13
- SOR/94-392, s. 7
- SOR/99-264, s. 16
- SOR/2008-322, s. 20
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 10](#1294865-1295333){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294865-1295333.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 29, no
person who is engaged in sport fishing shall, for the purpose of landing
a fish, use

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any net other than a landing net;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a tailer that exceeds 2 m in length;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a spring gaff; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of Atlantic salmon, any gaff.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2008-322, ss. 21, 34
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Fishing Location Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922272 .Subheading}

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall engage in sport fishing from

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bridge crossing a salmon river or an estuary
    thereof; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a motor boat in the waters of Saint-Jean River
    Wildlife Sanctuary or in that part of Matapédia-and-Patapédia-Rivers
    Wildlife Sanctuary between Causapscal River and the falls downstream
    from the Richard Salmon Pool situated near the boundary between
    Bonaventure and Matapédia counties.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall engage in sport fishing from a boat
  or from any other floating apparatus in the waters of the Sainte-Anne
  River wildlife sanctuary or of the Cap-Chat River, Dartmouth River,
  Matane River or Petit-Saguenay River controlled zones.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 14
- SOR/94-392, s. 8
- SOR/2001-51, s. 11
- SOR/2008-322, s. 22(F)
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 11(F)](#1294867-1295334){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294867-1295334.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-33-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 33"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922280 .Subheading}

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** No person shall fish in
any salmon river during the period beginning one hour after sunset and
ending one hour before sunrise other than from December 1 to the
Thursday preceding the fourth Friday in April in places where and during
periods when fishing for rainbow smelt is authorized.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-141, s. 1
- SOR/2001-51, s. 12
- SOR/2008-322, s. 23
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 63](#1294970-1295471){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294970-1295471.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Fishing Quotas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922284 .Subheading}

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  engaged in sport fishing shall catch and retain in any one day from
  the waters set out in an item of Schedule 2 more fish of a species set
  out in that item than the daily fishing quota set out in that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, in any one day,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 36, catch and retain more
    Atlantic salmon referred to in Schedule 2 from a salmon river set
    out in Schedule 6 than the highest daily fishing quota set out in
    Schedule 2 for that river; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} catch and release more Atlantic salmon referred to
    in Schedule 2 from a salmon river set out in Schedule 6 than the
    highest daily fishing quota set out in Schedule 2 for that river.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (1) and (2), fish
  caught and retained by a person referred to in paragraph 5(3)(a), (b)
  or (c) or, in the case of Atlantic salmon, fish caught and released by
  that person shall be counted as fish caught by the holder of the
  licence.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (1) and (2), fish
  caught and retained or Atlantic salmon caught and released in waters
  adjacent to waters set out in Schedules 2 and 6 shall be counted as
  fish from the waters set out in those Schedules.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-141, s. 2
- SOR/91-336, s. 2
- SOR/93-118, s. 15
- SOR/94-392, s. 9

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294869-1295336.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-35-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 35"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain in any year more than four Atlantic salmon and, of those, all
four can be small or three can be small and one may be large.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-264, s. 18
- SOR/2008-322, s. 34
- SOR/2018-55, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-36-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 36"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall, during any day, continue to fish for a species of fish in
  waters set out in Schedule 2 after the person has, during that day,
  caught and retained, in a body of water, the number of fish
  corresponding to the daily fishing quota stipulated in that Schedule
  for that species in respect of that body of water, unless the fishing
  continues in another body of water where the daily fishing quota
  stipulated for that species is higher.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, during any day, continue to fish in
  any salmon river set out in Schedule 6 and included in Area 1, 2, 3,
  18, 21, 27 or 28 after the person has, during that day, caught and
  retained or, if the daily quota set out in Schedule 2 applies in
  respect of catching and releasing, caught and released in that river a
  number of Atlantic salmon equal to the highest daily fishing quota of
  Atlantic salmon for the rivers in those Areas.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 16
- SOR/97-203, s. 11
- SOR/98-218, s. 8
- SOR/99-264, s. 19
- SOR/2008-322, s. 24
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 13](#1294871-1295338){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294871-1295338.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-37-20090401.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Possession Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922302 .Subheading}

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** No person shall have in
the person's possession, other than at the person's permanent residence,
any fish taken by sport fishing that are skinned, cut or packed in such
a manner that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the species cannot be readily identified;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of fish cannot be readily counted; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the length of fish cannot be readily measured where a
  length limit is applicable.

**[[38.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38.1 .sectionLabel}** Section 38 does not
apply to prohibit the possession of the equivalent in fillets of a
sauger or walleye that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} measures 30 cm or more in length and has been taken
  from the waters referred to in Schedule 2 and for which a minimum
  length limit of 30 cm has been set if the two fillets measure 20 cm or
  more in length with the skin adhering completely to the flesh; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} measures 35 cm or more in length and has been taken
  from the waters referred to in Schedule 2 and for which a minimum
  length limit of 35 cm has been set if the two fillets measure 23 cm or
  more in length with the skin adhering completely to the flesh.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-264, s. 20
- SOR/2008-322, s. 25
- SOR/2011-155, s. 2
- SOR/2012-47, s. 3
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 14](#1294873-1295341){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294873-1295341.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38.1-20120315.html "Link to previous version of section 38.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[38.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38.2 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section
29, no person shall have in their possession a fish taken by sport
fishing that is of a species set out in column 1 of Schedule 1 in waters
set out in column 2 during the close time set out in column 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-198, s. 11](#1489254-1492948){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1489254-1492948.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall have in their possession

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the waters in which the person is engaged in
    sport fishing, more fish than the daily quota set out in Schedule 2
    for that species for the waters in which the person is fishing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in a wildlife reserve or a communal wildlife area,
    more fish taken by sport fishing than the daily quota set out in
    Schedule 2 for that species for the waters of that wildlife reserve
    or communal wildlife area; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any place, for species of fish other than
    Atlantic salmon,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), more fish taken by
      sport fishing in an Area than the highest daily fishing quota set
      out in Schedule 2 for that species for the waters of that Area, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} more fish taken by sport fishing than the
      highest daily fishing quota set out in Schedule 2 for that species
      for the waters of all the Areas.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person may possess more lake trout or splake trout
  taken by sport fishing from an Area than the daily quota set out in
  Schedule 2 for those species in that Area if the lake trout or splake
  trout in excess of that quota are taken from waters of an outfitter
  and if the total number of lake trout and splake trout possessed by
  that person and taken in that Area does not exceed the highest daily
  quota for the waters of outfitters in that Area.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-336, s. 3
- SOR/98-218, s. 9
- SOR/99-264, s. 21
- SOR/2008-322, s. 25
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 15](#1294875-1295343){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294875-1295343.html"
  role="button"}
:::

:::

### [Length Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922322 .Subheading}

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** No person engaged in
sport fishing shall catch and retain or possess fish of a length that
does not comply with the length limit set out in an item of Schedule 2
from waters set out in that item.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, ss. 17, 22
- SOR/94-139, s. 1

:::

### [Salmon Registration]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922326 .Subheading}

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every
  person who catches and retains an Atlantic salmon shall, within 48
  hours after leaving the fishing location or immediately if requested
  to do so by a fishery officer, register the salmon in accordance with
  subsections (2) to (4).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person shall register the Atlantic salmon at the
  registration station that has been established for the body of water
  in which the salmon was caught by presenting their fishing licence and
  the salmon, either whole or gutted, and allowing a sample to be taken
  or an examination to be made for scientific purposes.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the registration station is not in operation, the
  person shall register the Atlantic salmon by using any
  self-registration procedure that is available at the station ---
  including by completing and signing any required form in accordance
  with the instructions given and depositing it in the indicated
  location --- or, if self-registration is not available, by using any
  remote registration procedure that has been implemented for the
  station.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If none of the methods of registration referred to in
  subsections (2) and (3) are available, the person shall register the
  Atlantic salmon using any remote registration procedure that is
  specified by the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/99-264, s. 23
- SOR/2008-322, ss. 26, 34
- [SOR/2021-115, s. 16(E)](#1294877-1295345){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}

  role="button"}
:::

:::

### [Burbot Fishing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922335 .Subheading}

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  holder of a licence set out in item 3 of Part 1 of Schedule 5 shall
  affix a valid tag issued with their licence to the identification
  marker for each of the night lines they are using and shall ensure
  that the night lines all rest continuously on the bottom of the water.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tag is not valid if it has been modified or
  altered in any manner that renders any information on the tag
  illegible.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-203, s. 13
- SOR/2001-51, s. 15

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Commercial Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-922345 .Part}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922346 .Subheading}

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part applies in respect of commercial fishing.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2003-176, s. 14\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-203, s. 15
- SOR/99-264, s. 25
- SOR/2003-176, s. 14
:::

### [Close Times and Fishing Quotas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922352 .Subheading}

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** No person shall engage in
commercial fishing under the authority of a licence referred to in
item 1, column 1, of Part 2 of Schedule 5 for fish of a species

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that may not be taken or transported under the
  conditions of the licence;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in waters in which a person may not engage in fishing
  for that species under the conditions of the licence;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} during a period in which a person may not engage in
  fishing for that species under the conditions of the licence;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} during a period in which that species may not be
  transported under the conditions of the licence;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} with a type, quantity or size of fishing gear that
  may not be used to fish for that species under the conditions of the
  licence; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} in a manner that may not be used to fish for that
  species under the conditions of the licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-51, s. 16
- SOR/2008-322, s. 29
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 47"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** No person who is engaged
in commercial fishing under the authority of a licence referred to in
item 1, column 1, of Part 2 of Schedule 5 shall catch and retain fish of
a species

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in a quantity greater than that which may be taken or
  transported in respect of that species under the conditions of the
  licence; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that, because of age, sex, stage of development,
  length or weight, may not be taken or transported under the conditions
  of the licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-51, s. 16
- SOR/2008-322, s. 30
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-48-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 48"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Identification of Fishing Gear]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922367 .Subheading}

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who engages in commercial fishing under the authority of a
  licence referred to in item 1, column 1, of Part 2 of Schedule 5 may
  use a fishing vessel in any tidal waters, or fishing gear in any
  waters, unless a valid identification plate that was provided with the
  licence is affixed to the vessel or gear in the manner indicated in
  the licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister or a Director, as the case may be, shall
  issue the identification plates referred to in subsection (1), or a
  commercial fisher's identification certificate, upon payment of an
  annual fee of \$10 per licence holder.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), an identification
  plate is valid if it has not been modified in any way.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 22, a commercial fisher's
  identification certificate is valid only if it has been signed by the
  licence holder and has not been modified in any way.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-118, s. 20
- SOR/97-203, s. 16
- SOR/99-264, s. 26
- SOR/2001-51, s. 17
- SOR/2005-269, s. 52
- SOR/2008-322, s. 31
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-49-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 49"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who is engaged in commercial fishing under the authority of a
  licence referred to in item 1, column 1, of Part 2 of Schedule 5 may
  catch or retain any species of fish referred to in subsection 3(1) in
  the waters referred to in that subsection during the close time
  beginning on January 1 and ending on the last day of February.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The close time referred to in subsection (1) is
  considered to be a separate close time for every species of fish
  referred to in subsection 3(1) found in the waters referred to in that
  subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-51, s. 18
- SOR/2008-322, s. 32
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-50-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

s. 27\]]{.Repealed}

SOR/2001-51, s. 19\]]{.Repealed}

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-922382 .Subheading}

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations shall
apply according to their terms before they are published in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

<p>The upper limit is determined by the lake named Lac Gagnon.</p>
<p>Between the lakes named Lac Gagnon and Lac Foucher, Lac Foucher and

id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBB0VD0AABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon
Rivers</span><sup>*</sup></a> Maccan, Rivière (Maccan River)
(Area 20)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by the
extension of two lines in the general direction of the shoreline over 1
km on both sides of the river.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by its source.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">51</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">MacDonald, Rivière (MacDonald
River) (Area 20)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by the
extension of two lines in the general direction of the shoreline over 1
km on both sides of the river.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the downstream side of the Route
Trans-Anticostienne bridge (49°45′27″N., 63°03′46″W.).</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">52</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Madeleine, Rivière (Madeleine
River) (Area 1)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by the
downstream side of the Route 132 bridge.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the southern boundary of the Parc
national de la Gaspésie.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">53</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBB0SD0AABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon
Rivers</span><sup>*</sup></a> Magpie, Rivière (Magpie River)
(Area 19)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by a
straight line perpendicular to the current joining the two banks at a
point 400 m downstream from the Route 138 bridge.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the hydro-electric dam.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Malbaie, Rivière (Malbaie
River) (South Shore, Malbaie Township) (Areas 1 and 21)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by a
straight line parallel to the CN Railway bridge 200 m downstream from
the bridge.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by its source.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">55</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Malbaie, Rivière (Malbaie
River) (North Shore) (Areas 21 and 27)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by a
straight line joining the Casgrain wharf to the mouth of the brook named
Ruisseau de la Côte à Pontage.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the dam named Barrage des Érables
located at point 47°53′31″N., 70°28′40″W.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">55(1)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Snigole, Rivière (Snigole

<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its
confluence with the river named Rivière Malbaie (North Shore).</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the falls located at point
47°42′43″N., 70°14′58″W.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">56</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Mars, Rivière à (à Mars River)
(Area 28)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by the

48°20′25″N., 70°52′39″W. and 48°20′17″N., 70°52′29″W.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the southern boundary of Dubuc
Township.</p></td>
</tr>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"

class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon

km on both sides of the river.</p>

</tr>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"

class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon

<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by a
