and 50°18′00″N., 63°58′00″W.</p>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">61(1)</td>

<p>The lakes named Lac à l’Île, Lac Marie-Claire, Lac Missu, Lac

class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon

(Area 19)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by two
straight lines joining the two banks and the island named Île
Menahkunakat:</p>
<ul>

point located on the western bank (50°11′32″N., 61°03′32″W.) and the
southwestern point of the island named Île Menahkunakat (50°11′57″N.,
61°02′41″W.); and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> a straight line joining the
northeastern point of Île Menahkunakat (50°12′22″N., 61°02′52″W.) and a
point located on the eastern bank (50°12′40″N., 61°03′15″W.).</p></li>
</ul>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the second falls located at
50°16′44″N., 61°11′23″W.</p></td>
</tr>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBB4AABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon
Rivers</span><sup>*</sup></a> Sainte-Marie, Rivière (Sainte-Marie River)
(Area 20)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by the
extension of two lines in the general direction of the shoreline over 1
km on both sides of the river.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by its source.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">99</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBB3AABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon
Rivers</span><sup>*</sup></a> Salmon Bay, Rivière de (Salmon Bay River)
(Area 19) (formerly au Saumon Brook)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by a
straight line joining points 51°27′26″N., 57°35′44″W. and 51°27′09″N.,
57°35′10″W.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by its source.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">100</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBB2AABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon
Rivers</span><sup>*</sup></a> Saumons, Rivière aux (aux Saumons River)
(Area 20)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by the
extension of two lines in the general direction of the shoreline over 1
km on both sides of the river.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by its source.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">101</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBB1AABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon
Rivers</span><sup>*</sup></a> Sheldrake, Rivière (Sheldrake River)
(Area 19)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by a
straight line perpendicular to the current joining the two banks at a
point 100 m downstream from the Route 138 bridge.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the falls situated 5.5 km upstream
(50°18′54″N., 64°54′49″W.).</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">101(1)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBBZAABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon
Rivers</span><sup>*</sup></a> Épinettes, Rivière d’ (d’Épinettes River)
(Area 19)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its
confluence with the river named Rivière Sheldrake.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the limit of the salmon run on the
river (50°19′13″N., 64°53′46″W.).</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">102</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Sud-Ouest, Rivière du (du
Sud-Ouest River) (Area 2)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its
mouth.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the downstream side of the Route 132
bridge.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Trinité, Rivière de la (de la
Trinité River) (Area 18)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by a
straight line joining the two banks at a point 175 m downstream from the
downstream side of the Route 138 bridge.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by its source.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(1)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Unnamed tributary of Rivière de
la Trinité (de la Trinité River) (Area 18)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its
confluence with the river named Rivière de la Trinité (49°24′28″N.,
67°26′25″W.).</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by a point 2 km upstream (49°23′59″N.,
67°26′55″W.).</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(2)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Unnamed tributary of Rivière de

<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its

67°27′14″W.).</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by a point 0.4 km upstream
(49°29′56″N., 67°27′09″W.).</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(3)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Unnamed tributary of Rivière de

<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(4)</td>

<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(5)</td>

</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(6)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Unnamed tributary of Rivière de

</tr>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(7)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Bilodeau, Ruisseau (Bilodeau

<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its

<p>The upper limit is determined by a point 7.4 km upstream
(49°26′58″N., 67°19′43″W.).</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(8)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Earle, Ruisseau (Earle Brook)
(Area 18)</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its
confluence with the river named Rivière de la Trinité.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the lake named Lac Earle.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">103(9)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Fafard, Ruisseau (Fafard Brook)

<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>The downstream limit is determined by its

<p>The upper limit is determined by a point 1.5 km upstream

</tr>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">106</td>

straight line joining points 51°00′00″N., 58°59′05″W. and 51°00′00″N.,
58°58′13″W.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the hydroelectric dam.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">107</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"

class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon

<p>The lakes named Lac Pike and Lac du Vieux Fort are included.</p>
<p>The upper limit is determined by the lake named Lac Fournel,

<tr>

</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">107(2)</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBBEAABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon

class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon

<p>The lakes named Lac Andilly (Ardilly), Lac d’Aune, Lac Couillard, Lac

<p>The upper limit is determined by the lake named Lac Caumont,

<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">109</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"

<p>The upper limit is determined by the lake named Lac Holt.</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">110</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom"><a href="#fn_608792-e_hq_6250"
id="fn_608792-e_hq_6250-ID0EBBBAABBA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for List of Salmon

and 50°15′59″N., 62°36′56″W.</p>

</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">111</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">York, Rivière (York River) (Area 1)</td>

<p>The upper limit is determined by the outlet of the lake named Lac

</tr>

</table>
